// FUNC 1800201e0 __alloca_probe
unsigned __int64 __fastcall _alloca_probe()
{
  unsigned __int64 result; // rax
  char *v1; // r10
  char *StackLimit; // r11
  char v3; // [rsp+18h] [rbp+8h] BYREF

  v1 = &v3 - result;
  if ( (unsigned __int64)&v3 < result )
    v1 = nullptr;
  StackLimit = (char *)NtCurrentTeb()->NtTib.StackLimit;
  if ( v1 < StackLimit )
  {
    LOWORD(v1) = (unsigned __int16)v1 & 0xF000;
    do
    {
      StackLimit -= 4096;
      *StackLimit = 0;
    }
    while ( v1 != StackLimit );
  }
  return result;
}

// FUNC 180020230 __isa_available_init

{

  int v6; // r8d

  int v15; // eax

  int v39; // eax
  unsigned __int64 v40; // rax

  _RAX = 0;
  __asm { cpuid }
  v5 = _RDX ^ 0x49656E69;
  v6 = _RCX ^ 0x6C65746E;
  v7 = _RBX;
  v8 = _RAX;
  _RAX = 1;

  v14 = _RCX;

    && ((_memset_nt_threshold = -1, v15 = _RAX & 0xFFF3FF0, _memset_fast_string_threshold = 0x8000, v15 == 67264)
     || v15 == 132704
     || v15 == 132720
     || (unsigned int)(v16 = v15 - 198224) <= 0x20 && (v17 = 0x100010001LL, _bittest64(&v17, (unsigned __int8)v16))) )
  {
    v18 = _favor | 1;
    _favor |= 1u;
  }
  else
  {
    v18 = _favor;
  }
  v19 = 0;
  v20 = 0;
  v21 = 0;
  v22 = 0;
  if ( v8 >= 7 )
  {
    _RAX = 7;
    __asm { cpuid }
    v20 = _RDX;

// FUNC 180022f60 __raise_securityfailure
BOOL __fastcall _raise_securityfailure(struct _EXCEPTION_POINTERS *ExceptionInfo)
{
  HANDLE CurrentProcess; // rax

  SetUnhandledExceptionFilter(nullptr);
  UnhandledExceptionFilter(ExceptionInfo);
  CurrentProcess = GetCurrentProcess();
  return TerminateProcess(CurrentProcess, 0xC0000409);
}
